# All closed Android issues: exhaustive evidence and parity audit

## Scope and result
**2630 of 2630 closed, non-pull-request issues** in [kiwix/kiwix-android](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues?q=is%3Aissue+state%3Aclosed) are classified below, spanning creation **2016-06-08 through 2026-10-27**. The listing endpoint returned **5072 closed objects** on 52 pages (`per_page=100`, `sort=created`, ascending) plus an explicitly fetched empty terminal page: **2630 issues + 2442 pull requests**. The PR objects are audited separately in [the closed-PR audit](android-all-closed-prs.md); the raw listing, every body/label/timestamp and the per-number classification are retained in [android-all-closed-issues.raw.json.xz](android-all-closed-issues.raw.json.xz).
Of the 2630 issues, **1146 carry the `bug` label** and are the individual focus of this audit; the remaining 1484 lack the `bug` label and are tabulated by closure category, including unlabelled bug-like reports. **This is an audit, not a claim of feature or bug-fix superset.** Disposition totals over all issues: **14 applicable, 57 already correct, 1938 not applicable, 621 unverified**.
No source code was changed by this audit. No app build, Android instrumentation run, emulator session, physical-device test, screen-reader run or runtime reproduction was executed. Statements about our tree are source inspection, not runtime certification. Line numbers can move; named symbols are the durable anchors.

### Disposition vocabulary
- **applicable** — the same observable gap exists in the Tauri tree at the cited path and needs a follow-up change.
- **already-correct** — a located implementation in our tree satisfies the behaviour the upstream fix established. The reference is the evidence; no runtime claim.
- **not-applicable** — the issue depends on an Android/JVM mechanism, an upstream build/CI process, a specific archive's content, or was closed without a fix. The reference names our replacement surface.
- **unverified** — a shared behaviour that could affect us, but the report lacks a reproducible archive/device fixture, or the outcome is engine-dependent. These are honest residuals, not silent passes.

> A row is never evidence that a feature works. `already-correct` means the source contract is present; `applicable` means a follow-up is required.

## Highest-risk actionable findings
These are the rows that would be real defects in our tree, with the precise missing behaviour. None was runtime-reproduced here.

| Priority / issues | Our implementation and honest gap | Concrete follow-up |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P1 [#4406](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4406), [#5202](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5202) | **Unverified packaging risk.** `src-tauri/gen/android/app/build.gradle.kts` declares SDK 36 but no `-Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384` link argument and no APK verification; bundled `libkiwix.so`/Tauri `.so` alignment is not asserted. On 16 KB-page Android 15+ devices an unaligned library prevents installation or crashes at load. | Inspect the release APK with `llvm-readelf -lW`/`zipalign -c -P 16`; add an explicit link arg and a CI check. Treat as release-blocking until measured. |
| P1 [#5132](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5132), [#4813](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4813) | **Applicable.** `src/reader-bridge.js:1173-1215` sets only `documentElement`/`body` colours and `color-scheme`. Archive-authored containers (`.mw-parser-output`, fixed white panels) keep their own backgrounds, so a dark setting does not restyle the article. Images are correctly not inverted. | Inject a scoped dark stylesheet for common article containers while preserving images/media, and add a real Wikipedia dark-mode fixture check. |
| P2 [#4814](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4814) | **Applicable.** `index.html:12` loads the app without an inline pre-paint theme; `App.tsx:664` applies `data-theme` only after the first snapshot IPC. A dark-theme launch can paint light first. | Add an early inline theme read (stored preference or native window background) and verify cold launch in dark mode. |
| P2 [#5207](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5207) | **Applicable.** The TOC `IconButton` in `Reader.tsx` is disabled only by `!frameReady`; an article with no outline still opens an empty panel. | Disable/hide the control when `toc.length === 0` after a validated ready message. |
| P2 [#4748](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4748) | **Applicable by design gap.** `acquisition.rs:1444-1450` exposes `resume_interrupted` only at explicit startup/settings; `mobile_policy.rs` denies/retires on network loss but no watcher resumes the job when connectivity returns. | Decide policy: automatic resume of interrupted (not paused) jobs on reconnect, or an explicit actionable prompt. Add a network-transition test. |
| P2 [#5118](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5118) | **Applicable/partial.** `Catalog.tsx` fetches a page and caches the response; there is no incremental catalogue synchronisation or reconciliation with installed books. | If parity is required, define a sync contract (ETag/304 per page, merge, stale markers) rather than re-downloading. |
| P2 [#4433](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4433), [#4467](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4467), [#4473](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4473) | **Applicable acceptance gap.** Named controls/landmarks exist, but no TalkBack/VoiceOver traversal, focus order, live-region or touch-target audit was run. | Run a device screen-reader pass over library, search, reader toolbar, TOC, notes and downloads. |
| P2 [#4553](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4553) | **Applicable by design.** Speech capabilities are retired when the window/installation retires; Android 16 background playback does not auto-resume. | State the limitation in UI/help, or implement an explicitly user-started foreground continuation. |
| P2 [#2671](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/2671) | **Applicable.** `Reader.tsx` `documentPath` and `server.rs` `split_location` split a location at the first `?`/`#` before encoding. A search hit whose libzim path literally contains `?` (German/English Wikipedia articles) therefore resolves to the prefix article. Percent-encoded links from inside articles work, which matches the upstream report. | Encode the whole libzim path from search results (or carry an explicit encoded path) so literal delimiters survive; add the `?`-title fixture to search/bookmark/history tests. |
| P2 [#4965](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4965) | **Applicable/unverified.** `mobile_import_incoming`/`mobile_rebase.rs` handle retained provider descriptors, but the Android 16 move-to-library path has no device evidence. | Exercise the exact Android 16 DocumentsUI move flow and assert the archive remains readable after relaunch. |

### P1/P2 issues needing an immediate code fix
- **#4406/#5202 (P1, release-blocking until measured):** verify and, if needed, force 16 KB alignment for every bundled native library. This is the only finding that can make the Android package uninstallable on current hardware.
- The remaining P2 items are behaviour/acceptance gaps, not data-loss or security defects. No P1 data-loss or security finding was established by this audit.

## Upstream fixes already correct in our tree
These rows matter most for the stated goal: the bug was real upstream, and our implementation already avoids it. Each cites the located contract.

| Upstream issues | Behaviour upstream fixed | Our located contract |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [#5164](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5164), [#5168](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5168), [#5171](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5171), [#4194](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4194) | Notes were file-backed; notes for different articles overwrote each other, dots in URLs collided, sharing read stale text, deleting left orphan files. | `store.rs::Bookmark` stores `note` as a field keyed by `(book_id, path)`; `service.rs::save_bookmark` upserts that identity; `remove_bookmark` deletes by id. No note files exist. |
| [#5176](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5176), [#5181](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5181), [#4728](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4728) | Bookmarking a redirect alias deleted both bookmarks; deleting one bookmark removed another book's entry; sidebar navigation deleted a bookmark instead of saving. | Upsert identity is exact `(book_id,path)`; removal is id-scoped; save never removes a different row (`service.rs` `save_bookmark`). |
| [#5149](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5149), [#5190](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5190) | History lost a page when the same URL differed only by letter case; one day split into several headers after a language change. | `store.rs::history_resource_identity` preserves path case and query; `service.rs::record_visit` dedupes by that identity; `Collections.tsx` renders a flat list with a per-entry timestamp, not locale-grouped day headers. |
| [#5188](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5188), [#50](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/50), [#760](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/760) | Articles with `+`, `%` or non-ASCII characters in the path opened blank. | `server.rs::normalize_location`/`encode_path` percent-encode with `NON_ALPHANUMERIC` (`+`→`%2B`); decoding is percent-only, never form decoding; encoded delimiters round-trip. A literal `?`/`#` in a libzim path is still split (see #2671). |
| [#4954](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4954) | OPDS search query parameters were not URL-encoded. | `Catalog.tsx:190` sets `q` via `URLSearchParams`; `catalog.rs` preserves filters through `query_pairs_mut` when deriving `start`. |
| [#4701](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4701) | Online-library pagination lost pages/offsets. | `catalog.rs::parse_catalog` validates `totalResults`/`startIndex`/`itemsPerPage`, derives the next offset, and has lower-bound/explicit-next tests. |
| [#2347](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/2347) | Range responses returned 200 instead of 206. | `server.rs:778-792` parses ranges, returns `206 PARTIAL_CONTENT` with `Content-Range`, and `416` with `bytes */size` for unsatisfiable ranges. |
| [#309](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/309), [#863](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/863), [#1064](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/1064) | TOC/find navigation failed inside collapsed sections. | `reader-bridge.js::reveal`/`revealSearchableContent` opens `DETAILS`, clears collapse classes and restores display before heading/find navigation. |
| [#4510](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4510), [#3605](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/3605), [#3691](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/3691) | Split ZIM archives were not listed/opened; bookmarks crashed. | `library_files.rs` admits only an exact `.zimaa` cohort (primary + every part), rejects incomplete/extra parts, and keeps one book id. |
| [#4357](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4357) | Only one ZIM per title was shown. | `store.rs` keys books by archive id; all installed editions are retained. |
| [#5011](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/5011), [#4558](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4558) | Empty online library showed a blank screen. | `Catalog.tsx` renders an explicit `EmptyState` with retry guidance. |
| [#4843](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4843) | Donate link did not reach the donation page. | `Settings.tsx:612` opens `https://kiwix.org/en/donate/`. |
| [#3682](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/3682), [#3762](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/3762) | Opening a note/history entry whose archive is missing crashed or misbehaved. | `Collections.tsx` marks the archive unavailable and disables Open; no live archive is dereferenced. |
| [#3899](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/3899) | Duplicate history/note insert threw a SQLite constraint exception. | History/bookmarks are JSON vectors with upsert semantics; no unique constraint to violate. |
| [#4265](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4265) | ZIMs were removed from the library automatically. | `library_maintenance` only refreshes `available` flags; removal requires `delete_book`. |
| [#4937](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4937) | Language selector showed no languages after a failed facet load. | `Catalog.tsx:468-478` renders an explicit facet error with a `Try again` action; facets are empty only while loading or when the producer supplies none. |

## Deliberate architectural exclusions
- **Legacy service-worker archives** ([#3234](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/3234), [#3854](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/3854), [#3878](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/3878), [#2561](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/2561), [#2661](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/2661)): replay is not implemented. `Catalog.tsx` detects the producer tag `_sw:yes` and discloses incompatibility; blob workers in normal archives still work.
- **Android shell mechanics** (Activities/Fragments, notifications, widgets, WorkManager, intents, DocumentProvider UI, Play Store/custom-app packaging, in-app review): replaced by the Tauri webview shell and the native plugin; these reports cannot regress our tree as written. The user-visible equivalents that do exist are tracked by their own rows.
- **Uninstall deletes app-private ZIM copies** ([#4150](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-android/issues/4150)): an OS storage policy, not a defect we can code around for managed copies; direct external-provider reading remains the mitigation.
- **Upstream CI/release/translation chores**: not runtime behaviour and not carried into our build.

## Regression guard list
Upstream bugs whose regression in our tree would be a real defect. Each guard names a concrete check a future change can run. Guards are ordered by severity; none is currently known to fail except where the applicable table says so.

| # | Guard | Upstream evidence | Concrete check |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| G1 | Note identity is `(archive id, full article path)`; dots, slashes, `%`, `+`, `#` and query strings never collide; deleting one note leaves no side file. | #5164/#5168/#5171/#4194 | Create notes on `A.html`, `A.B.html`, `A%2FB.html`, `A?x=1` in one archive and the same paths in another; assert 8 distinct rows survive restart and deleting one removes exactly one. |
| G2 | Bookmark upsert/removal is id-scoped and never cascades across archives or redirect aliases. | #5176/#5181/#4728 | Bookmark article X, its redirect alias, and the same path in a second archive; delete one id; assert the other rows and both library entries remain. |
| G3 | History identity preserves case and query; no duplicate rows after language change; missing archives are labelled, not dereferenced. | #5149/#5190/#3762/#3682 | Visit `/A`, `/a`, `/A?q=1`; change UI locale; assert three rows, stable titles, and an unavailable archive cannot be opened. |
| G4 | Article paths with `+`, `%`, `#`, `?`, spaces and non-ASCII round-trip through search results, bookmarks, history, export and reload. | #5188/#3755/#50/#2671 | Open each character fixture from search and from history; assert non-blank content and identical location after reload. |
| G5 | Range serving returns 206 with correct `Content-Range`; suffix/open-ended/nonzero ranges and 416 are correct. | #2347/#2532 | `curl -r 0-0`, `-r 50-`, `-r -10`, and an out-of-range request against a large media entry; compare bytes and status. |
| G6 | Split ZIM cohorts are admitted only when complete and are one book; incomplete/extra parts are rejected. | #4510/#3605/#3691/#3861 | Import `.zimaa` alone, then with all parts, then with an extra part; assert rejection, one book, and a bookmark that survives. |
| G7 | Download jobs survive pause/restart/network loss; partial bytes are never published; final checksum gates import. | #695/#232/#433/#4140/#4138 | Pause at >0 bytes, kill the process, restart, resume; assert exact remainder, one published file, matching SHA-256, no duplicate job. |
| G8 | Interrupted transfers are not silently auto-resumed unless the user opted in; a reconnect does not corrupt a paused job. | #4748/#1147/#3919 | Kill connectivity mid-transfer, restore it, and assert the documented policy (explicit prompt/resume) with intact staging. |
| G9 | The library never removes or rewrites a book implicitly; maintenance only flips availability. | #4265/#4132/#4148 | Start with a missing archive; run maintenance; assert the book row and metadata remain and only `available` changes. |
| G10 | Dark/light/sepia appearance never inverts images and is applied before first paint. | #153/#1242/#1625/#1652/#4814 | Cold-launch in dark mode; assert no light flash, and open an image-heavy article asserting pixel colours are unchanged. |
| G11 | TOC/find navigation reveals collapsed ancestors and the TOC control is inert when no outline exists. | #309/#863/#1064/#5207 | Open an article with collapsed sections; navigate by TOC and find; assert the target is visible. Open a no-heading article; assert the control is disabled. |
| G12 | Find-in-page is scoped to the active tab/frame and survives switching archives. | #4200/#4377/#4224 | Open two tabs, run find in each, switch, and assert the result belongs to the visible document only. |
| G13 | OPDS query/filter/pagination parameters are encoded and preserved across pages. | #4954/#4701/#957 | Search with spaces, `&`, `+`, non-ASCII; follow next page; assert the producer receives the same decoded query and no page is skipped. |
| G14 | Legacy `_sw:yes` archives are disclosed before acquisition and never claimed to work offline. | #3234/#3854/#362 | Point the catalogue at a `_sw:yes` entry; assert the warning appears before download and the archive is not presented as readable. |
| G15 | Read-aloud stops/retires with its tab and never resumes an expired capability. | #3740/#4553/#5173 | Start speech, close the tab, reopen; assert stop, no stale callback, and a fresh explicit start works. |
| G16 | Every bundled native library is 16 KB-page aligned and the APK passes alignment verification. | #4406/#5202 | Run `zipalign -c -P 16` and `llvm-readelf -lW` on the release APK; assert `LOAD` alignment ≥ 0x4000 for arm64. |
| G17 | Library/catalog empty and error states are explicit, never a blank screen. | #5011/#4558/#4883 | Simulate offline and empty feed; assert a named empty/error state with retry. |
| G18 | Deleting a bookmark/history entry removes exactly that row; no cross-archive cascade. | #5181/#5176 | Seed two archives with same-path bookmarks; delete one; assert the other and its library entry remain. |
| G19 | Import/move never overwrites an existing archive or follows a symlink out of the chosen directory. | #695/#818/#4965 | Import into a directory containing a sentinel and a symlink; assert no clobber and no escape. |
| G20 | Archive close/reopen is reference-counted; no stale reader serves a deleted or replaced file. | #859/#1034/#1415 | Open, delete/replace the file, reopen; assert an error or new content, never stale bytes. |
| G21 | Search cancellation stops snippet work and a new query is not queued behind the old one. | #1791 (Apple)/#1344/#2082 | Type a query, cancel, type another; assert no post-cancel snippets and bounded latency. |
| G22 | CJK and single-character title/full-text queries return the same hits as the engine for a fixture archive. | #3587/#3896/#3891/#3631 | Run a Chinese fixture with 1-, 2- and 3-character queries; compare with `zimsearch`/libzim output. |
| G23 | Uninstall/relaunch policy for managed copies is explicit; external-provider references survive when possible. | #4150/#4655/#4965 | Install, import from a provider, uninstall, reinstall; assert documented outcome and no silent loss of user-original files. |

## What could not be determined
- No Android device/emulator execution was available to this audit, so every `applicable` and `unverified` row remains a source-level judgement. The 16 KB packaging risk (P1) is the only finding that could block installation.
- Archive-content failures (specific Wikipedia/Zimit/Wikihow/PDF/EPUB fixtures) cannot be attributed to our reader without the named ZIM and engine versions; those rows stay `unverified` rather than being called fixed.
- Android JVM crash reports (NPE, `Input dispatching timed out`, Dagger/ObjectBox, Activity leaks) describe a runtime our shell does not use; they are `not-applicable`, not evidence that the corresponding user-visible symptom cannot occur in another form.
- The listing is a snapshot: issues closed after retrieval, edited bodies, and comments/timelines were not fetched. Closure reasons come from labels and titles; where a label was absent the row says so.
- Per-row dispositions outside the overridden high-risk set inherit a cluster default. The cluster rules and overrides are reproducible from the raw artifact; they are triage, not individual reproduction.
